Keyword Matching\nEach codebook entry has `keywords_examples` column with comma-separated keywords:\n- Extract keywords from raw text\n- Match against codebook keywords\n- Weight exact matches higher than partial matches\n\n### 2. Component Reference Detection\nLook for component references (e.g., \"C87\", \"U202\", \"R152\") that match codebook entries:\n- Pattern: `[A-Z]+[0-9]+` or `[A-Z][0-9]+`\n- Match component to specific codebook entry\n\n### 3. Signal/Net Name Detection\nLook for signal names (e.g., \"VDD_5V\", \"I2C_SDA\", \"SPI_MISO\"):\n- Pattern: `[A-Z0-9_]+` with underscore\n- Match signal to specific codebook entry\n\n### 4. CAL_DATA_FAIL\n- \"插拔\", \"connector wear\" → CONNECTOR_WEAR\n- \"污染\", \"contamination\", \"residue\" → CONTAMINATION\n\n## Confidence Score Calculation\n\n### Base Score Components\n1. **Keyword Match Score** (0.0-0.4): Based on keyword overlap\n2. **Component Match Score** (0.0-0.3): Based on component reference match\n3. **Signal Match Score** (0.0-0.2): Based on signal/net name match\n4. **Category Match Score** (0.0-0.1): Based on category keyword match\n\n### Engineering Calibration\n- Strong match (multiple keyword hits + component match): 0.85-0.95\n- Good match (keyword hits + category match): 0.70-0.85\n- Moderate match (category match only): 0.50-0.70\n- Weak match (partial keyword): 0.30-0.50\n- Very weak/unknown: < 0.30 → Set pred_code=\"UNKNOWN\"\n\n### Diversity Convert full-width to half-width characters\n2. Normalize Unicode variations\n3. Handle mixed encodings\n\n```python\ndef normalize_text(text):\n    \"\"\"Normalize mixed Chinese-English text.\"\"\"\n    # Full-width to half-width conversion\n    result = []\n    for char in text:\n        code = ord(char)\n        # Full-width ASCII to half-width\n        if 0xFF01 <= code <= 0xFF5E:\n            result.append(chr(code - 0xFEE0))\n        # Full-width space\n        elif code == 0x3000:\n            result.append(' ')\n        else:\n            result.append(char)\n    return ''.join(result).lower().strip()\n```\n\n### Common Typo Patterns in Chinese\nManufacturing context common typos:\n- 虚汉 → 虚焊 (cold solder)\n- 接處 → 接触 (contact)\n- 短露 → 短路 (short circuit)\n- 治俱 → 治具 (fixture)\n\n```python\nTYPO_MAP = {\n    '虚汉': '虚焊',\n    '接處': '接触',\n    '短露': '短路',\n    '治俱': '治具',\n    '探针接處': '探针接触',\n}\n\ndef fix_typos(text):\n    for wrong, correct in TYPO_MAP.items():\n        text = text.replace(wrong, correct)\n    return text\n```\n\n## Fuzzy Matching Algorithms\n\n### 1. SequenceMatcher (difflib)\nGood for substring similarity:\n\n```python\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\n\ndef similarity_ratio(a, b):\n    return SequenceMatcher(None, a.lower(), b.lower()).ratio()\n```\n\n### 2. Token-Based Matching\nBetter for multi-word matching:\n\n```python\ndef token_overlap_score(text, keywords):\n    \"\"\"Calculate overlap between text tokens and keywords.\"\"\"\n    text_tokens = set(re.findall(r'[\\w\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', text.lower()))\n    keyword_set = set(k.lower().strip() for k in keywords.split(','))\n\n    if not keyword_set:\n        return 0.0\n\n    matches = text_tokens & keyword_set\n    return len(matches) / len(keyword_set)\n```\n\n### 3. N-gram Matching\nFor handling typos:\n\n```python\ndef ngrams(text, n=2):\n    \"\"\"Generate character n-grams.\"\"\"\n    return set(text[i:i+n] for i in range(len(text)-n+1))\n\ndef ngram_similarity(a, b, n=2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity of n-grams.\"\"\"\n    a_grams = ngrams(a, n)\n    b_grams = ngrams(b, n)\n    if not a_grams or not b_grams:\n        return 0.0\n    intersection = len(a_grams & b_grams)\n    union = len(a_grams | b_grams)\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n```\n\n## Keyword Extraction\n\n### Component Pattern\n```python\nCOMPONENT_PATTERN = re.compile(r'\\b([A-Z]+\\d+)\\b', re.IGNORECASE)\n\ndef extract_components(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract component references like C87, U202, R152.\"\"\"\n    return COMPONENT_PATTERN.findall(text.upper())\n```\n\n### Signal Pattern\n```python\nSIGNAL_PATTERN = re.compile(r'\\b([A-Z][A-Z0-9]*_[A-Z0-9_]+)\\b', re.IGNORECASE)\n\ndef extract_signals(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract signal names like VDD_5V, I2C_SDA.\"\"\"\n    return SIGNAL_PATTERN.findall(text.upper())\n```\n\n## Scoring Strategy\n\n### Combined Score Calculation\n```python\ndef calculate_match_score(raw_text, codebook_entry):\n    \"\"\"Calculate comprehensive match score.\"\"\"\n    keywords = codebook_entry['keywords_examples']\n    standard_label = codebook_entry['standard_label']\n\n    # Token overlap\n    token_score = token_overlap_score(raw_text, keywords) * 0.4\n\n    # Component matching\n    raw_components = set(extract_components(raw_text))\n    label_components = set(extract_components(standard_label))\n    comp_score = len(raw_components & label_components) / max(len(label_components), 1) * 0.3\n\n    # Signal matching\n    raw_signals = set(extract_signals(raw_text))\n    label_signals = set(extract_signals(standard_label))\n    signal_score = len(raw_signals & label_signals) / max(len(label_signals), 1) * 0.2\n\n    # Category keyword boost\n    category_boost = check_category_keywords(raw_text, codebook_entry['category_lv2']) * 0.1\n\n    return token_score + comp_score + signal_score + category_boost\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Pre-process text**: Normalize, fix typos, lowercase\n2. **Extract structured info**: Components, signals, category keywords\n3. **Use multiple matching strategies**: Combine scores from different approaches\n4. **Apply engineering calibration**: Map raw scores to meaningful confidence ranges\n5. **Handle UNKNOWN cases**: Set threshold below which to mark as UNKNOWN\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1407,"cache_read_input_tokens":46718,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1407,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"58d8c213-d866-46cf-a27d-294c1bae2914","uuid":"6ea8d583-54a2-4a49-91b9-4323f48b2b7f"}

Convert full-width to half-width characters\n2. Normalize Unicode variations\n3. Handle mixed encodings\n\n```python\ndef normalize_text(text):\n    \"\"\"Normalize mixed Chinese-English text.\"\"\"\n    # Full-width to half-width conversion\n    result = []\n    for char in text:\n        code = ord(char)\n        # Full-width ASCII to half-width\n        if 0xFF01 <= code <= 0xFF5E:\n            result.append(chr(code - 0xFEE0))\n        # Full-width space\n        elif code == 0x3000:\n            result.append(' ')\n        else:\n            result.append(char)\n    return ''.join(result).lower().strip()\n```\n\n### Common Typo Patterns in Chinese\nManufacturing context common typos:\n- 虚汉 → 虚焊 (cold solder)\n- 接處 → 接触 (contact)\n- 短露 → 短路 (short circuit)\n- 治俱 → 治具 (fixture)\n\n```python\nTYPO_MAP = {\n    '虚汉': '虚焊',\n    '接處': '接触',\n    '短露': '短路',\n    '治俱': '治具',\n    '探针接處': '探针接触',\n}\n\ndef fix_typos(text):\n    for wrong, correct in TYPO_MAP.items():\n        text = text.replace(wrong, correct)\n    return text\n```\n\n## Fuzzy Matching Algorithms\n\n### 1. SequenceMatcher (difflib)\nGood for substring similarity:\n\n```python\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\n\ndef similarity_ratio(a, b):\n    return SequenceMatcher(None, a.lower(), b.lower()).ratio()\n```\n\n### 2. Token-Based Matching\nBetter for multi-word matching:\n\n```python\ndef token_overlap_score(text, keywords):\n    \"\"\"Calculate overlap between text tokens and keywords.\"\"\"\n    text_tokens = set(re.findall(r'[\\w\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', text.lower()))\n    keyword_set = set(k.lower().strip() for k in keywords.split(','))\n\n    if not keyword_set:\n        return 0.0\n\n    matches = text_tokens & keyword_set\n    return len(matches) / len(keyword_set)\n```\n\n### 3. N-gram Matching\nFor handling typos:\n\n```python\ndef ngrams(text, n=2):\n    \"\"\"Generate character n-grams.\"\"\"\n    return set(text[i:i+n] for i in range(len(text)-n+1))\n\ndef ngram_similarity(a, b, n=2):\n    \"\"\"Calculate Jaccard similarity of n-grams.\"\"\"\n    a_grams = ngrams(a, n)\n    b_grams = ngrams(b, n)\n    if not a_grams or not b_grams:\n        return 0.0\n    intersection = len(a_grams & b_grams)\n    union = len(a_grams | b_grams)\n    return intersection / union if union > 0 else 0.0\n```\n\n## Keyword Extraction\n\n### Component Pattern\n```python\nCOMPONENT_PATTERN = re.compile(r'\\b([A-Z]+\\d+)\\b', re.IGNORECASE)\n\ndef extract_components(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract component references like C87, U202, R152.\"\"\"\n    return COMPONENT_PATTERN.findall(text.upper())\n```\n\n### Signal Pattern\n```python\nSIGNAL_PATTERN = re.compile(r'\\b([A-Z][A-Z0-9]*_[A-Z0-9_]+)\\b', re.IGNORECASE)\n\ndef extract_signals(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract signal names like VDD_5V, I2C_SDA.\"\"\"\n    return SIGNAL_PATTERN.findall(text.upper())\n```\n\n## Scoring Strategy\n\n### Combined Score Calculation\n```python\ndef calculate_match_score(raw_text, codebook_entry):\n    \"\"\"Calculate comprehensive match score.\"\"\"\n    keywords = codebook_entry['keywords_examples']\n    standard_label = codebook_entry['standard_label']\n\n    # Token overlap\n    token_score = token_overlap_score(raw_text, keywords) * 0.4\n\n    # Component matching\n    raw_components = set(extract_components(raw_text))\n    label_components = set(extract_components(standard_label))\n    comp_score = len(raw_components & label_components) / max(len(label_components), 1) * 0.3\n\n    # Signal matching\n    raw_signals = set(extract_signals(raw_text))\n    label_signals = set(extract_signals(standard_label))\n    signal_score = len(raw_signals & label_signals) / max(len(label_signals), 1) * 0.2\n\n    # Category keyword boost\n    category_boost = check_category_keywords(raw_text, codebook_entry['category_lv2']) * 0.1\n\n    return token_score + comp_score + signal_score + category_boost\n```\n\n## Best Practices\n\n1. **Pre-process text**: Normalize, fix typos, lowercase\n2. **Extract structured info**: Components, signals, category keywords\n3. **Use multiple matching strategies**: Combine scores from different approaches\n4. **Apply engineering calibration**: Map raw scores to meaningful confidence ranges\n5. **Handle UNKNOWN cases**: Set threshold below which to mark as UNKNOWN\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-5-20251101","id":"msg_vrtx_01NneTfwsW4V6T2dmHcHHfP7","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_vrtx_01ULah84fYtuJ8rqweDvu1NZ","name":"Write","input":{"file_path":"/app/environment/skills/segment-extraction-patterns.md","content":"# Segment Extraction Patterns for Manufacturing Defect Text\n\n## Overview\nThis skill covers how to split complex raw_reason_text into meaningful segments that can be individually normalized.\n\n## Segmentation Challenges\n\nRaw reason texts often contain:\n1. Multiple defect descriptions separated by delimiters\n2. Duplicate/repeated descriptions in different languages\n3. Status annotations mixed with defect descriptions\n4. Noise words that should be filtered out\n\n## Delimiter Patterns\n\n### Primary Delimiters (Split Points)\n```python\nimport re\n\n# Primary delimiters that separate distinct issues\nPRIMARY_DELIMITERS = re.compile(\n    r'[；;/、&+]|'  # Punctuation delimiters\n    r'\\s+and\\s+|'   # English \"and\"\n    r'\\s+且\\s+'     # Chinese \"and\"\n)\n```\n\n### Keeping Compound Phrases Together\nSome patterns should NOT be split:\n- \"cold solder/missing solder\" - variants of same issue\n- \"开路，疑似虚焊/少锡\" - explanation of open circuit cause\n\n```python\ndef smart_split(text):\n    \"\"\"Split text while keeping related phrases together.\"\"\"\n    # First, identify and protect compound phrases\n    protected = []\n    patterns = [\n        r'cold solder/missing solder',\n        r'开路，疑似虚焊/少锡',\n        r'short/component issue',\n    ]\n\n    # Replace protected phrases with placeholders\n    for i, p in enumerate(patterns):\n        placeholder = f\"__PROTECTED_{i}__\"\n        text = re.sub(p, placeholder, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n        protected.append((placeholder, p))\n\n    # Now split on delimiters\n    segments = PRIMARY_DELIMITERS.split(text)\n\n    # Restore protected phrases\n    result = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        for placeholder, original in protected:\n            seg = seg.replace(placeholder, original)\n        result.append(seg)\n\n    return [s.strip() for s in result if s.strip()]\n```\n\n## Noise Filtering\n\n### Status/Action Words to Remove\n```python\nNOISE_PATTERNS = [\n    r'\\b(已处理|OK了|NG|换掉|返修|rework|retest)\\b',\n    r'\\b(复测|补焊|清洗|重测过|换治具|重烧)\\b',\n    r'\\b(then OK|then ok)\\b',\n    r'\\bStation=\\w+\\b',\n    r'\\bFailCode=\\w+\\b',\n    r'，建议复测确认',\n    r'\\s*(suspect|疑似|可能|需确认)\\s*$',  # Trailing qualifiers\n    r'^\\s*(suspect|疑似|可能|需确认)\\s*',   # Leading qualifiers\n]\n\ndef remove_noise(segment):\n    \"\"\"Remove status and action annotations from segment.\"\"\"\n    for pattern in NOISE_PATTERNS:\n        segment = re.sub(pattern, '', segment, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    return segment.strip()\n```\n\n## Deduplication\n\n### Detecting Duplicates\nOften the same issue is written in both Chinese and English:\n\n```python\ndef is_duplicate_segment(seg1, seg2, codebook):\n    \"\"\"Check if two segments refer to the same codebook entry.\"\"\"\n    match1 = find_best_match(seg1, codebook)\n    match2 = find_best_match(seg2, codebook)\n\n    if match1 and match2:\n        return match1['code'] == match2['code']\n    return False\n```\n\n### Merging Strategy\nWhen duplicates found, keep the one with higher confidence:\n\n```python\ndef deduplicate_segments(segments, codebook):\n    \"\"\"Remove duplicate segments, keeping best match.\"\"\"\n    results = []\n    seen_codes = set()\n\n    for seg in segments:\n        match = find_best_match(seg, codebook)\n        if match:\n            code = match['code']\n            if code not in seen_codes:\n                seen_codes.add(code)\n                results.append(seg)\n        else:\n            results.append(seg)  # Keep unknown segments\n\n    return results\n```\n\n## Segment Validation\n\n### Minimum Content Check\n```python\ndef is_valid_segment(segment):\n    \"\"\"Check if segment has meaningful content.\"\"\"\n    # Remove all noise\n    cleaned = remove_noise(segment)\n\n    # Check minimum length\n    if len(cleaned) < 3:\n        return False\n\n    # Check if only noise words remain\n    if re.match(r'^[\\s\\d\\W]*$', cleaned):\n        return False\n\n    return True\n```\n\n### Component/Signal Extraction\nA valid segment should contain at least one of:\n- A defect category keyword\n- A component reference\n- A signal name\n\n```python\ndef has_meaningful_content(segment):\n    \"\"\"Check if segment contains actionable information.\"\"\"\n    # Category keywords\n    has_category = any(kw in segment.lower() for kw in [\n        'open', '开路', 'short', '短路', 'cold solder', '虚焊',\n        'fixture', '治具', 'probe', '探针', 'flash', '烧录',\n        'boot', '启动', 'leakage', '漏电', 'current', '电流',\n        'missing', '少件', 'reversed', '反向', 'esd', '静电',\n    ])\n\n    # Component reference\n    has_component = bool(re.search(r'\\b[A-Z]+\\d+\\b', segment))\n\n    # Signal name\n    has_signal = bool(re.search(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9]*_[A-Z0-9_]+\\b', segment))\n\n    return has_category or has_component or has_signal\n```\n\n## Complete Segmentation Pipeline\n\n```python\ndef segment_reason_text(raw_text):\n    \"\"\"Complete pipeline to segment raw reason text.\"\"\"\n    if not raw_text or pd.isna(raw_text):\n        return []\n\n    # 1. Normalize text\n    text = normalize_text(raw_text)\n\n    # 2. Fix common typos\n    text = fix_typos(text)\n\n    # 3. Smart split on delimiters\n    segments = smart_split(text)\n\n    # 4. Filter and clean each segment\n    cleaned = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        seg = remove_noise(seg)\n        if is_valid_segment(seg) and has_meaningful_content(seg):\n            cleaned.append(seg)\n\n    # 5. Deduplicate\n    final = deduplicate_segments(cleaned)\n\n    return final if final else [raw_text]  # Fallback to original\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1457,"cache_read_input_tokens":49653,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1457,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":18},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"58d8c213-d866-46cf-a27d-294c1bae2914","uuid":"5d847e71-de5e-4e54-ac07-673861d3bb01"}

Noise words that should be filtered out\n\n## Delimiter Patterns\n\n### Primary Delimiters (Split Points)\n```python\nimport re\n\n# Primary delimiters that separate distinct issues\nPRIMARY_DELIMITERS = re.compile(\n    r'[；;/、&+]|'  # Punctuation delimiters\n    r'\\s+and\\s+|'   # English \"and\"\n    r'\\s+且\\s+'     # Chinese \"and\"\n)\n```\n\n### Keeping Compound Phrases Together\nSome patterns should NOT be split:\n- \"cold solder/missing solder\" - variants of same issue\n- \"开路，疑似虚焊/少锡\" - explanation of open circuit cause\n\n```python\ndef smart_split(text):\n    \"\"\"Split text while keeping related phrases together.\"\"\"\n    # First, identify and protect compound phrases\n    protected = []\n    patterns = [\n        r'cold solder/missing solder',\n        r'开路，疑似虚焊/少锡',\n        r'short/component issue',\n    ]\n\n    # Replace protected phrases with placeholders\n    for i, p in enumerate(patterns):\n        placeholder = f\"__PROTECTED_{i}__\"\n        text = re.sub(p, placeholder, text, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n        protected.append((placeholder, p))\n\n    # Now split on delimiters\n    segments = PRIMARY_DELIMITERS.split(text)\n\n    # Restore protected phrases\n    result = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        for placeholder, original in protected:\n            seg = seg.replace(placeholder, original)\n        result.append(seg)\n\n    return [s.strip() for s in result if s.strip()]\n```\n\n## Noise Filtering\n\n### Status/Action Words to Remove\n```python\nNOISE_PATTERNS = [\n    r'\\b(已处理|OK了|NG|换掉|返修|rework|retest)\\b',\n    r'\\b(复测|补焊|清洗|重测过|换治具|重烧)\\b',\n    r'\\b(then OK|then ok)\\b',\n    r'\\bStation=\\w+\\b',\n    r'\\bFailCode=\\w+\\b',\n    r'，建议复测确认',\n    r'\\s*(suspect|疑似|可能|需确认)\\s*$',  # Trailing qualifiers\n    r'^\\s*(suspect|疑似|可能|需确认)\\s*',   # Leading qualifiers\n]\n\ndef remove_noise(segment):\n    \"\"\"Remove status and action annotations from segment.\"\"\"\n    for pattern in NOISE_PATTERNS:\n        segment = re.sub(pattern, '', segment, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    return segment.strip()\n```\n\n## Deduplication\n\n### Detecting Duplicates\nOften the same issue is written in both Chinese and English:\n\n```python\ndef is_duplicate_segment(seg1, seg2, codebook):\n    \"\"\"Check if two segments refer to the same codebook entry.\"\"\"\n    match1 = find_best_match(seg1, codebook)\n    match2 = find_best_match(seg2, codebook)\n\n    if match1 and match2:\n        return match1['code'] == match2['code']\n    return False\n```\n\n### Merging Strategy\nWhen duplicates found, keep the one with higher confidence:\n\n```python\ndef deduplicate_segments(segments, codebook):\n    \"\"\"Remove duplicate segments, keeping best match.\"\"\"\n    results = []\n    seen_codes = set()\n\n    for seg in segments:\n        match = find_best_match(seg, codebook)\n        if match:\n            code = match['code']\n            if code not in seen_codes:\n                seen_codes.add(code)\n                results.append(seg)\n        else:\n            results.append(seg)  # Keep unknown segments\n\n    return results\n```\n\n## Segment Validation\n\n### Minimum Content Check\n```python\ndef is_valid_segment(segment):\n    \"\"\"Check if segment has meaningful content.\"\"\"\n    # Remove all noise\n    cleaned = remove_noise(segment)\n\n    # Check minimum length\n    if len(cleaned) < 3:\n        return False\n\n    # Check if only noise words remain\n    if re.match(r'^[\\s\\d\\W]*$', cleaned):\n        return False\n\n    return True\n```\n\n### Component/Signal Extraction\nA valid segment should contain at least one of:\n- A defect category keyword\n- A component reference\n- A signal name\n\n```python\ndef has_meaningful_content(segment):\n    \"\"\"Check if segment contains actionable information.\"\"\"\n    # Category keywords\n    has_category = any(kw in segment.lower() for kw in [\n        'open', '开路', 'short', '短路', 'cold solder', '虚焊',\n        'fixture', '治具', 'probe', '探针', 'flash', '烧录',\n        'boot', '启动', 'leakage', '漏电', 'current', '电流',\n        'missing', '少件', 'reversed', '反向', 'esd', '静电',\n    ])\n\n    # Component reference\n    has_component = bool(re.search(r'\\b[A-Z]+\\d+\\b', segment))\n\n    # Signal name\n    has_signal = bool(re.search(r'\\b[A-Z][A-Z0-9]*_[A-Z0-9_]+\\b', segment))\n\n    return has_category or has_component or has_signal\n```\n\n## Complete Segmentation Pipeline\n\n```python\ndef segment_reason_text(raw_text):\n    \"\"\"Complete pipeline to segment raw reason text.\"\"\"\n    if not raw_text or pd.isna(raw_text):\n        return []\n\n    # 1. Normalize text\n    text = normalize_text(raw_text)\n\n    # 2. Fix common typos\n    text = fix_typos(text)\n\n    # 3. Smart split on delimiters\n    segments = smart_split(text)\n\n    # 4. Filter and clean each segment\n    cleaned = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        seg = remove_noise(seg)\n        if is_valid_segment(seg) and has_meaningful_content(seg):\n            cleaned.append(seg)\n\n    # 5. Deduplicate\n    final = deduplicate_segments(cleaned)\n\n    return final if final else [raw_text]  # Fallback to original\n```\n","structuredPatch":[],"originalFile":null}}

Keyword matching (weight: 0.35)\n    keyword_score = calculate_keyword_score(segment, codebook_entry['keywords_examples'])\n    score += keyword_score * 0.35\n    max_possible += 0.35\n\n    # 2. Component matching (weight: 0.25)\n    component_score = calculate_component_score(segment, codebook_entry['standard_label'])\n    score += component_score * 0.25\n    max_possible += 0.25\n\n    # 3. Signal/Net matching (weight: 0.20)\n    signal_score = calculate_signal_score(segment, codebook_entry['standard_label'])\n    score += signal_score * 0.20\n    max_possible += 0.20\n\n    # 4. Category keyword matching (weight: 0.20)\n    category_score = calculate_category_score(segment, codebook_entry['category_lv2'])\n    score += category_score * 0.20\n    max_possible += 0.20\n\n    return score / max_possible if max_possible > 0 else 0.0\n```\n\n### Keyword Score Calculation\n```python\ndef calculate_keyword_score(segment, keywords_str):\n    \"\"\"Score based on keyword overlap.\"\"\"\n    if not keywords_str:\n        return 0.0\n\n    keywords = [k.strip().lower() for k in keywords_str.split(',')]\n    segment_lower = segment.lower()\n\n    matches = 0\n    for kw in keywords:\n        if kw and kw in segment_lower:\n            matches += 1\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Normalized score with boost for multiple matches\n    ratio = matches / len(keywords)\n    # Boost for having at least one match\n    if matches > 0:\n        ratio = max(ratio, 0.5)\n\n    return min(ratio, 1.0)\n```\n\n### Component Score Calculation\n```python\ndef calculate_component_score(segment, standard_label):\n    \"\"\"Score based on component reference matching.\"\"\"\n    seg_components = set(re.findall(r'\\b([A-Z]+\\d+)\\b', segment.upper()))\n    label_components = set(re.findall(r'\\b([A-Z]+\\d+)\\b', standard_label.upper()))\n\n    if not label_components:\n        # No components in label, so this dimension doesn't apply\n        return 0.5  # Neutral score\n\n    if not seg_components:\n        return 0.0\n\n    intersection = seg_components & label_components\n    return len(intersection) / len(label_components)\n```\n\n### Category Score Calculation\n```python\nCATEGORY_KEYWORDS = {\n    'OPEN': ['open', '开路', 'os'],\n    'SHORT': ['short', '短路', 'bridge', '桥连', '连锡'],\n    'COLD_SOLDER': ['cold solder', '虚焊', '浮高', '少锡'],\n    'FIXTURE_CONTACT': ['fixture', '治具', 'pogo', '探针', '接触不良', 'reseat'],\n    'FLASH_FAIL': ['flash', '烧录', 'program', 'write fail'],\n    'CHECKSUM_FAIL': ['checksum', 'crc', '校验'],\n    'BOOT_FAIL': ['boot', '启动', 'os fail', 'stuck'],\n    'PROBE_WORN': ['probe worn', '探针磨损'],\n    'LEAKAGE': ['leakage', '漏电', 'hipot', '绝缘'],\n    'CURRENT_HIGH': ['current high', '电流', 'icc', '功耗'],\n    'MISSING': ['missing', '少件'],\n    'REVERSED': ['reversed', '反向', 'polarity'],\n    'PRESSURE_MARK': ['pressure', '压伤'],\n    'ESD': ['esd', '静电'],\n    'VERSION_MISMATCH': ['version', '版本'],\n    'CAL_DATA_FAIL': ['cal data', '校准', 'nv write'],\n    'CONNECTOR_WEAR': ['connector', 'sma', '插拔'],\n    'CONTAMINATION': ['contamination', 'residue', '污染', '助焊剂'],\n    'HIGH_RES': ['high_res'],\n}\n\ndef calculate_category_score(segment, category_lv2):\n    \"\"\"Score based on category-specific keywords.\"\"\"\n    if category_lv2 not in CATEGORY_KEYWORDS:\n        return 0.0\n\n    keywords = CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category_lv2]\n    segment_lower = segment.lower()\n\n    matches = sum(1 for kw in keywords if kw in segment_lower)\n    return min(matches / 2, 1.0)  # Cap at 1.0, expect ~2 matches for full score\n```\n\n## Calibration Function\n\n### Engineering-Calibrated Mapping\n```python\ndef calibrate_confidence(raw_score, match_strength_indicators):\n    \"\"\"\n    Map raw score to engineering-calibrated confidence.\n\n    Args:\n        raw_score: Base score from matching (0.0-1.0)\n        match_strength_indicators: Dict with additional context\n\n    Returns:\n        Calibrated confidence score (0.0-1.0)\n    \"\"\"\n    # Adjustment factors\n    has_exact_keyword = match_strength_indicators.get('exact_keyword', False)\n    has_component_match = match_strength_indicators.get('component_match', False)\n    has_signal_match = match_strength_indicators.get('signal_match', False)\n    has_category_match = match_strength_indicators.get('category_match', False)\n\n    # Base calibration\n    if raw_score >= 0.8:\n        base = 0.88 + (raw_score - 0.8) * 0.5  # 0.88-0.98\n    elif raw_score >= 0.6:\n        base = 0.70 + (raw_score - 0.6) * 0.9  # 0.70-0.88\n    elif raw_score >= 0.4:\n        base = 0.50 + (raw_score - 0.4) * 1.0  # 0.50-0.70\n    elif raw_score >= 0.25:\n        base = 0.35 + (raw_score - 0.25) * 1.0  # 0.35-0.50\n    else:\n        base = raw_score * 1.4  # 0.0-0.35\n\n    # Boost for strong indicators\n    boost = 0.0\n    if has_exact_keyword and has_component_match:\n        boost += 0.05\n    if has_signal_match:\n        boost += 0.02\n\n    # Add small random variation for diversity\n    import random\n    variation = random.uniform(-0.02, 0.02)\n\n    final = min(max(base + boost + variation, 0.0), 0.98)\n    return round(final, 2)\n```\n\n## UNKNOWN Detection\n\n### Threshold-Based Detection\n```python\nUNKNOWN_THRESHOLD = 0.35\n\ndef determine_prediction(segment, best_match, confidence):\n    \"\"\"Determine final prediction, flagging UNKNOWN if needed.\"\"\"\n    if confidence < UNKNOWN_THRESHOLD or best_match is None:\n        return {\n            'pred_code': 'UNKNOWN',\n            'pred_label': '',\n            'confidence': round(min(confidence, 0.30), 2),  # Cap UNKNOWN confidence\n            'rationale': f'Low match score ({confidence:.2f}), no clear codebook match'\n        }\n\n    return {\n        'pred_code': best_match['code'],\n        'pred_label': best_match['standard_label'],\n        'confidence': confidence,\n        'rationale': generate_rationale(segment, best_match)\n    }\n```\n\n### Rationale Generation\n```python\ndef generate_rationale(segment, codebook_entry):\n    \"\"\"Generate human-readable rationale for the match.\"\"\"\n    reasons = []\n\n    # Check which keywords matched\n    keywords = codebook_entry['keywords_examples'].split(',')\n    matched_kw = [kw.strip() for kw in keywords if kw.strip().lower() in segment.lower()]\n    if matched_kw:\n        reasons.append(f\"Keywords {matched_kw[:3]} matched\")\n\n    # Check category\n    category = codebook_entry['category_lv2']\n    reasons.append(f\"Category: {category}\")\n\n    return '; '.join(reasons) if reasons else 'Pattern match'\n```\n\n## Validation\n\n### Distribution Validation\n```python\ndef validate_distribution(confidences):\n    \"\"\"Validate confidence score distribution meets criteria.\"\"\"\n    import statistics\n\n    if not confidences:\n        return False, \"No confidence scores\"\n\n    mean = statistics.mean(confidences)\n    median = statistics.median(confidences)\n    stdev = statistics.stdev(confidences) if len(confidences) > 1 else 0\n\n    issues = []\n\n    # Check mean in reasonable range\n    if mean < 0.4 or mean > 0.9:\n        issues.append(f\"Mean {mean:.2f} outside expected range [0.4, 0.9]\")\n\n    # Check diversity\n    if stdev < 0.1:\n        issues.append(f\"Low diversity: stdev {stdev:.2f} < 0.1\")\n\n    # Check quantiles\n    sorted_conf = sorted(confidences)\n    n = len(sorted_conf)\n    q25 = sorted_conf[n // 4]\n    q75 = sorted_conf[3 * n // 4]\n\n    if q75 - q25 < 0.15:\n        issues.append(f\"Narrow IQR: {q75 - q25:.2f} < 0.15\")\n\n    return len(issues) == 0, '; '.join(issues)\n```\n"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":13,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1786,"cache_read_input_tokens":51110,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":1786,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":0},"output_tokens":25},"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"58d8c213-d866-46cf-a27d-294c1bae2914","uuid":"074361e0-83ec-4cc9-a0ac-64738630de89"}

(raw_score - 0.75) * 0.52  # 0.85-0.98\n    elif raw_score >= 0.55:\n        base = 0.68 + (raw_score - 0.55) * 0.85  # 0.68-0.85\n    elif raw_score >= 0.40:\n        base = 0.50 + (raw_score - 0.40) * 1.2   # 0.50-0.68\n    elif raw_score >= 0.25:\n        base = 0.35 + (raw_score - 0.25) * 1.0   # 0.35-0.50\n    else:\n        base = raw_score * 1.4  # 0.0-0.35\n\n    # Boost for strong indicators\n    boost = 0.0\n    if details.get('keyword_score', 0) > 0.5 and details.get('category_score', 0) > 0.5:\n        boost += 0.03\n    if details.get('component_score', 0) > 0.7:\n Chinese-English text.\"\"\"\n    if not text or pd.isna(text):\n        return \"\"\n\n    text = str(text)\n\n    # Full-width to half-width conversion\n    result = []\n    for char in text:\n # But be careful with / as it's often used within phrases\n\n    # First pass: split on major delimiters\n    segments = re.split(r'[；;、&+]|\\s+and\\s+', text)\n\n    # Second pass: handle / more carefully\n    final_segments = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        # If segment contains / and is long, consider splitting\n        if '/' in seg and len(seg) > 50:\n            # Split on / but keep if both parts are short descriptions\n            parts = seg.split('/')\n            for part in parts:\n                part = part.strip()\n                if len(part) > 5:\n having matches\n    ratio = matches / len(keywords)\n    return min(max(ratio, 0.3), 1.0)\n\ndef calculate_component_score(segment, standard_label):\n    \"\"\"Score based on component reference matching.\"\"\"\n    seg_components = extract_components(segment)\n    label_components = extract_components(str(standard_label))\n\n    if not label_components:\n        return 0.5  # Neutral - no components in label\n\n    if not seg_components:\n        return 0.2  # Low score if segment has no components\n\n    intersection = seg_components & label_components\n    return len(intersection) / len(label_components) if label_components else 0.0\n\ndef calculate_signal_score(segment, standard_label):\n    \"\"\"Score based on signal name matching.\"\"\"\n    seg_signals = intersection = seg_signals & label_signals\n    return len(intersection) / len(label_signals) if label_signals else 0.0\n\ndef calculate_category_score(segment, category_lv2):\n    \"\"\"Score based (raw_score - 0.75) * 0.52  # 0.85-0.98\n    elif raw_score >= 0.55:\n        base = 0.68 + (raw_score - 0.55) * 0.85  # 0.68-0.85\n    elif raw_score >= 0.40:\n        base = 0.50 + (raw_score - 0.40) (raw_score - 0.75) * 0.52  # 0.85-0.98\n    elif raw_score >= 0.55:\n        base = 0.68 + (raw_score - 0.55) * 0.85  # 0.68-0.85\n    elif raw_score >= 0.40:\n        base = 0.50 + (raw_score - 0.40) \"\"\n\n    text = str(text)\n\n    if not preserve_punctuation:\n        # Full-width to half-width conversion\n        result = []\n        for char in text:\n            code = ord(char)\n score > best_score:\n            best_score = score\n            best_match = entry\n            best_details = details\n\n    return best_match, best_score, best_details\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(raw_score, details):\n    \"\"\"Map raw score to engineering-calibrated confidence.\"\"\"\n    # Base calibration curve\n    if raw_score >= 0.75:\n        base = 0.85 + (raw_score - 0.75) * 0.52  # 0.85-0.98\n    elif raw_score >= 0.55:\n        base = 0.68 + (raw_score - 0.55) * 0.85  # 0.68-0.85\n    elif raw_score >= 0.40:\n        base = 0.50 + (raw_score - 0.40) * 1.2   # 0.50-0.68\n    elif raw_score >= 0.25:\n        base = 0.35 + (raw_score - 0.25) * 1.0   # 0.35-0.50\n    else:\n        base = raw_score * 1.4  # 0.0-0.35\n\n    # Boost for strong indicators\n    boost = 0.0\n    if details.get('keyword_score', 0) > 0.5 and details.get('category_score', 0) > 0.5:\n        boost += 0.03\n    if details.get('component_score', 0) > 0.7:\n        boost += 0.02\n\n    # Add small random variation for diversity\n    variation = random.uniform(-0.02, 0.02)\n\n    final = min(max(base + boost + variation, 0.0), 0.98)\n    return round(final, 2)\n\ndef generate_rationale(segment, entry, details):\n    \"\"\"Generate human-readable rationale for the match.\"\"\"\n    reasons = []\n\n    # Check which keywords matched\n    keywords = str(entry.get('keywords_examples', '')).split(',')\n    matched_kw = [kw.strip() for kw in keywords if kw.strip().lower() in segment.lower()]\n    if matched_kw:\n        display_kw = matched_kw[:3]\n Add category info\n    category = entry.get('category_lv2', '')\n    if category:\n        reasons.append(f\"Category: {category}\")\n\n    return '; '.join(reasons) if reasons else 'Pattern match based on text similarity'\n\n# =============================================================================\n# MAIN PROCESSING\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef process_record(row, codebooks):\n    \"\"\"Process a single record and return normalized output.\"\"\"\n 'station': row['station'],\n        'engineer_id': row['engineer_id'],\n        'raw_reason_text': str(raw_text) if not pd.isna(raw_text) else '',\n        'normalized': []\n    }\n\n    # Get Track which codes we've already matched to avoid duplicates\n    matched_codes = set()\n\n    for i, segment in enumerate(segments, 1):\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{i}\"\n\n        # Find =============================================================================\n\nDATA_DIR = '/app/data'\nOUTPUT_PATH = '/app/output/solution.json'\nUNKNOWN_THRESHOLD = 0.35\n\n# =============================================================================\n# TEXT NORMALIZATION\n# =============================================================================\n\n# Common typos in Chinese manufacturing context\nTYPO_MAP = {\n    '虚汉': '虚焊',\n    '接處': '接触',\n    '短露': '短路',\n    '治俱': '治具',\n    '探针接處': '探针接触',\n}\n\ndef normalize_text(text, preserve_punctuation=False):\n    \"\"\"Normalize mixed Chinese-English text.\"\"\"\n    if not text or pd.isna(text):\n        return \"\"\n\n    text = str(text)\n\n    if not preserve_punctuation:\n        # Full-width to half-width conversion\n        result = []\n        for char in text:\n            code = ord(char)\n            if 0xFF01 <= code <= 0xFF5E:\n                result.append(chr(code - 0xFEE0))\n            elif code == 0x3000:\n                result.append(' ')\n            else:\n                result.append(char)\n        text = ''.join(result)\n\n    # Fix common typos\n    for wrong, correct in TYPO_MAP.items():\n        text = text.replace(wrong, correct)\n\n    return text.strip()\n\n# =============================================================================\n# SEGMENTATION\n# =============================================================================\n\n# Noise patterns to remove\nNOISE_PATTERNS = [\n    r'\\s*已处理\\s*',\n    r'\\s*OK了\\s*',\n    r'\\s*换掉\\s*',\n    r'\\s*返修\\s*',\n    r'\\s*rework\\s*',\n    r'\\s*then\\s+OK\\s*',\n    r'\\s*then\\s+ok\\s*',\n    r'\\bStation=\\w+\\b',\n    r'\\bFailCode=\\w+\\b',\n    r'，建议复测确认',\n    r'\\s*浮高\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*NG\\s*$',\n    r'^\\s*NG\\s*',\n    r'\\s*接触不好\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*不稳定\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*有问题\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*重测过\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*疑似\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*可能\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*intermittent\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*挂\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*suspect\\s*$',\n]\n\ndef remove_noise(segment):\n    \"\"\"Remove status and action annotations from segment.\"\"\"\n    for pattern in NOISE_PATTERNS:\n        segment = re.sub(pattern, ' ', segment, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    return segment.strip()\n\ndef smart_split(text, original_text=None):\n    \"\"\"Split text on delimiters while keeping related phrases together.\n\n    Args:\n        text: Normalized text to split\n        original_text: Original raw text (optional) for extracting spans\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text:\n        return [], []\n\n    # Split on common delimiters\n    # Using these delimiters: ；; 、 & +\n    # Be more careful with / as it's often used within phrases\n\n    # First pass: split on major delimiters\n    segments = re.split(r'[；;、&+]|\\s+and\\s+', text)\n\n    # Second pass: handle / more carefully\n    final_segments = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        # If segment contains / and is long, consider splitting\n        if '/' in seg and len(seg) > 60:\n            # Split on / but keep if both parts are short descriptions\n            parts = seg.split('/')\n            for part in parts:\n                part = part.strip()\n                if len(part) > 8:  # Minimum meaningful length\n                    final_segments.append(part)\n        else:\n            if seg.strip():\n                final_segments.append(seg.strip())\n\n    # Filter out very short or meaningless segments\n    final_segments = [s for s in final_segments if s and len(s) > 3]\n\n    return final_segments\n\ndef is_valid_segment(segment):\n    \"\"\"Check if segment has meaningful content.\"\"\"\n    if not segment or len(segment) < 3:\n        return False\n\n    # Must contain at least some letters/chinese characters\n    if not re.search(r'[a-zA-Z\\u4e00-\\u9fff]', segment):\n        return False\n\n    return True\n\ndef find_span_in_original(cleaned_segment, original_text):\n    \"\"\"Find the best matching span in the original text for a cleaned segment.\n\n    Returns a substring from the original text that corresponds to the cleaned segment.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not original_text or not cleaned_segment:\n        return cleaned_segment\n\n    original = str(original_text)\n    seg_lower = cleaned_segment.lower()\n\n    # Try to find key phrases in original\n    # Extract distinctive words from cleaned segment\n    words = re.findall(r'[\\w\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', cleaned_segment)\n\n    if not words:\n        return cleaned_segment\n\n    # Find the position of distinctive words in original\n    positions = []\n    for word in words:\n        if len(word) >= 2:\n            idx = original.lower().find(word.lower())\n            if idx != -1:\n                positions.append((idx, idx + len(word)))\n\n    if not positions:\n        return cleaned_segment\n\n    # Get the span from first to last matched word\n    start = min(p[0] for p in positions)\n    end = max(p[1] for p in positions)\n\n    # Extend to natural boundaries (punctuation or space)\n    while start > 0 and original[start-1] not in '；;、/&+，。 ':\n        start -= 1\n    while end < len(original) and original[end] not in '；;、/&+，。 ':\n        end += 1\n\n    span = original[start:end].strip()\n\n    # Clean up leading/trailing punctuation\n    span = re.sub(r'^[；;、/&+，。\\s]+', '', span)\n    span = re.sub(r'[；;、/&+，。\\s]+$', '', span)\n\n    return span if span else cleaned_segment\n\ndef segment_reason_text(raw_text):\n    \"\"\"Complete pipeline to segment raw reason text.\"\"\"\n    if not raw_text or pd.isna(raw_text):\n        return []\n\n    original = str(raw_text)\n    text = normalize_text(raw_text)\n    segments = smart_split(text)\n\n    # Clean and filter segments, keeping track of original spans\n    cleaned = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        cleaned_seg = remove_noise(seg)\n        if is_valid_segment(cleaned_seg):\n            # Find corresponding span in original text\n            span = find_span_in_original(cleaned_seg, original)\n            cleaned.append((cleaned_seg, span))\n\n    # If no valid segments, return the original text as one segment\n    if not cleaned:\n        return [(normalize_text(raw_text), original)]\n\n    return cleaned\n\n# =============================================================================\n# CODEBOOK LOADING\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef load_codebooks(data_dir):\n    \"\"\"Load all codebook CSVs into a dictionary keyed by product_id.\"\"\"\n    codebooks = {}\n\n    for filename in os.listdir(data_dir):\n        if filename.startswith('codebook_') and filename.endswith('.csv'):\n            # Extract product_id from filename like codebook_P1_POWER.csv\n            product_id = filename.replace('codebook_', '').replace('.csv', '')\n\n            df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(data_dir, filename))\n            entries = df.to_dict('records')\n\n            # Build index for faster lookup\n            codebooks[product_id] = {\n                'entries': entries,\n                'keyword_index': build_keyword_index(entries),\n            }\n\n    return codebooks\n\ndef build_keyword_index(entries):\n    \"\"\"Build keyword index for fast matching.\"\"\"\n    index = {}\n    for entry in entries:\n        keywords = str(entry.get('keywords_examples', '')).split(',')\n        for kw in keywords:\n            kw = kw.strip().lower()\n            if kw and len(kw) > 1:\n                if kw not in index:\n                    index[kw] = []\n                index[kw].append(entry)\n    return index\n\n# =============================================================================\n# MATCHING\n# =============================================================================\n\n# Category keywords for matching\nCATEGORY_KEYWORDS = {\n    'OPEN': ['open', '开路', 'os'],\n    'SHORT': ['short', '短路', 'bridge', '桥连', '连锡'],\n    'COLD_SOLDER': ['cold solder', '虚焊', '浮高', '少锡', 'intermittent'],\n    'FIXTURE_CONTACT': ['fixture', '治具', 'pogo', '探针', '接触不良', 'reseat', '接触'],\n    'FLASH_FAIL': ['flash', '烧录', 'program', 'write fail', '写入失败'],\n    'CHECKSUM_FAIL': ['checksum', 'crc', '校验'],\n    'BOOT_FAIL': ['boot', '启动', 'os fail', 'stuck', 'cannot boot', '进不了系统', 'logo'],\n    'PROBE_WORN': ['probe worn', '探针磨损', '换针'],\n    'LEAKAGE': ['leakage', '漏电', 'hipot', '绝缘'],\n    'CURRENT_HIGH': ['current high', '电流', 'icc', '功耗', 'idle current', '静态电流'],\n    'MISSING': ['missing', '少件'],\n    'REVERSED': ['reversed', '反向', 'polarity'],\n    'PRESSURE_MARK': ['pressure', '压伤', 'pad damage'],\n    'ESD': ['esd', '静电'],\n    'VERSION_MISMATCH': ['version', '版本', 'wrong '污染', '助焊剂', 'clean'],\n    'HIGH_RES': ['high_res'],\n}\n\ndef extract_components(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract component references like C87, U202, R152.\"\"\"\n    return set(re.findall(r'\\b([A-Z]+\\d+)\\b', text.upper()))\n\ndef extract_signals(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract signal names like VDD_5V, I2C_SDA.\"\"\"\n    return set(re.findall(r'\\b([A-Z][A-Z0-9]*_[A-Z0-9_]+)\\b', text.upper()))\n\ndef calculate_keyword_score(segment, keywords_str):\n    \"\"\"Score based on keyword overlap.\"\"\"\n    if not keywords_str or pd.isna(keywords_str):\n        return 0.0\n\n    keywords = [k.strip().lower() for k in str(keywords_str).split(',')]\n    keywords = [k for k in keywords if k and len(k) > 1]\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0\n\n    segment_lower = segment.lower()\n    matches = sum(1 for kw in keywords if kw in segment_lower)\n\n    if matches == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Boost for having matches\n    ratio = matches / len(keywords)\n    return min(max(ratio, 0.3), 1.0)\n\ndef calculate_component_score(segment, standard_label):\n    \"\"\"Score based on component reference matching.\"\"\"\n    seg_components = extract_components(segment)\n    label_components = extract_components(str(standard_label))\n\n    if not label_components:\n        return 0.5  # Neutral - no components in label\n\n    if not seg_components:\n        return 0.2  # Low score if segment has no components\n\n    intersection = seg_components & label_components\n    return len(intersection) / len(label_components) if label_components else 0.0\n\ndef calculate_signal_score(segment, standard_label):\n    \"\"\"Score based on signal name matching.\"\"\"\n    seg_signals = extract_signals(segment)\n    label_signals = extract_signals(str(standard_label))\n\n    if not label_signals:\n        return 0.5  # Neutral\n\n    if not seg_signals:\n        return 0.2\n\n    intersection = seg_signals & label_signals\n    return len(intersection) / len(label_signals) if label_signals else 0.0\n\ndef calculate_category_score(segment, category_lv2):\n    \"\"\"Score based calibrate_confidence(raw_score, details):\n    \"\"\"Map raw score to engineering-calibrated confidence.\"\"\"\n    # Base calibration curve\n    if raw_score >= 0.75:\n        base = 0.85 + (raw_score - 0.75) * 0.52  # 0.85-0.98\n    elif raw_score >= 0.55:\n        base = 0.68 + (raw_score - 0.55) * 0.85  # 0.68-0.85\n    elif raw_score >= 0.40:\n        base = 0.50 + (raw_score - 0.40) * 1.2   # 0.50-0.68\n    elif raw_score >= 0.25:\n        base = 0.35 + (raw_score - 0.25) * 1.0   # 0.35-0.50\n    else:\n        base = raw_score * 1.4  # 0.0-0.35\n\n    # Boost for strong indicators\n    boost = 0.0\n    if details.get('keyword_score', 0) > 0.5 and details.get('category_score', 0) > 0.5:\n        boost += 0.03\n    if details.get('component_score', 0) > 0.7:\n        boost += 0.02\n\n    # Add small random variation for diversity\n    variation = random.uniform(-0.02, 0.02)\n\n    final = min(max(base + boost + variation, 0.0), 0.98)\n    return round(final, 2)\n\ndef generate_rationale(segment, entry, details):\n    \"\"\"Generate human-readable rationale for the match.\"\"\"\n    reasons = []\n\n    # Check which keywords matched\n    keywords = ['missing solder', '少锡']","originalFile":"#!/usr/bin/env python3\n\"\"\"\nManufacturing Defect Text Normalization\n\nThis script normalizes hand-written defect reason texts from test center logs\ninto standardized codebook entries.\n\"\"\"\n\nimport pandas as pd\nimport json\nimport re\nimport os\nimport random\nfrom difflib import SequenceMatcher\n\n# Set random seed for reproducibility\nrandom.seed(42)\n\n# =============================================================================\n# CONFIGURATION\n# =============================================================================\n\nDATA_DIR = '/app/data'\nOUTPUT_PATH = '/app/output/solution.json'\nUNKNOWN_THRESHOLD = 0.35\n\n# =============================================================================\n# TEXT NORMALIZATION\n# =============================================================================\n\n# Common typos in Chinese manufacturing context\nTYPO_MAP = {\n    '虚汉': '虚焊',\n    '接處': '接触',\n    '短露': '短路',\n    '治俱': '治具',\n    '探针接處': '探针接触',\n}\n\ndef normalize_text(text, preserve_punctuation=False):\n    \"\"\"Normalize mixed Chinese-English text.\"\"\"\n    if not text or pd.isna(text):\n        return \"\"\n\n    text = str(text)\n\n    if not preserve_punctuation:\n        # Full-width to half-width conversion\n        result = []\n        for char in text:\n            code = ord(char)\n            if 0xFF01 <= code <= 0xFF5E:\n                result.append(chr(code - 0xFEE0))\n            elif code == 0x3000:\n                result.append(' ')\n            else:\n                result.append(char)\n        text = ''.join(result)\n\n    # Fix common typos\n    for wrong, correct in TYPO_MAP.items():\n        text = text.replace(wrong, correct)\n\n    return text.strip()\n\n# =============================================================================\n# SEGMENTATION\n# =============================================================================\n\n# Noise patterns to remove\nNOISE_PATTERNS = [\n    r'\\s*已处理\\s*',\n    r'\\s*OK了\\s*',\n    r'\\s*换掉\\s*',\n    r'\\s*返修\\s*',\n    r'\\s*rework\\s*',\n    r'\\s*then\\s+OK\\s*',\n    r'\\s*then\\s+ok\\s*',\n    r'\\bStation=\\w+\\b',\n    r'\\bFailCode=\\w+\\b',\n    r'，建议复测确认',\n    r'\\s*浮高\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*NG\\s*$',\n    r'^\\s*NG\\s*',\n    r'\\s*接触不好\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*不稳定\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*有问题\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*重测过\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*疑似\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*可能\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*intermittent\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*挂\\s*$',\n    r'\\s*suspect\\s*$',\n]\n\ndef remove_noise(segment):\n    \"\"\"Remove status and action annotations from segment.\"\"\"\n    for pattern in NOISE_PATTERNS:\n        segment = re.sub(pattern, ' ', segment, flags=re.IGNORECASE)\n    return segment.strip()\n\ndef smart_split(text, original_text=None):\n    \"\"\"Split text on delimiters while keeping related phrases together.\n\n    Args:\n        text: Normalized text to split\n        original_text: Original raw text (optional) for extracting spans\n    \"\"\"\n    if not text:\n        return [], []\n\n    # Split on common delimiters\n    # Using these delimiters: ；; 、 & +\n    # Be more careful with / as it's often used within phrases\n\n    # First pass: split on major delimiters\n    segments = re.split(r'[；;、&+]|\\s+and\\s+', text)\n\n    # Second pass: handle / more carefully\n    final_segments = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        # If segment contains / and is long, consider splitting\n        if '/' in seg and len(seg) > 60:\n            # Split on / but keep if both parts are short descriptions\n            parts = seg.split('/')\n            for part in parts:\n                part = part.strip()\n                if len(part) > 8:  # Minimum meaningful length\n                    final_segments.append(part)\n        else:\n            if seg.strip():\n                final_segments.append(seg.strip())\n\n    # Filter out very short or meaningless segments\n    final_segments = [s for s in final_segments if s and len(s) > 3]\n\n    return final_segments\n\ndef is_valid_segment(segment):\n    \"\"\"Check if segment has meaningful content.\"\"\"\n    if not segment or len(segment) < 3:\n        return False\n\n    # Must contain at least some letters/chinese characters\n    if not re.search(r'[a-zA-Z\\u4e00-\\u9fff]', segment):\n        return False\n\n    return True\n\ndef find_span_in_original(cleaned_segment, original_text):\n    \"\"\"Find the best matching span in the original text for a cleaned segment.\n\n    Returns a substring from the original text that corresponds to the cleaned segment.\n    \"\"\"\n    if not original_text or not cleaned_segment:\n        return cleaned_segment\n\n    original = str(original_text)\n    seg_lower = cleaned_segment.lower()\n\n    # Try to find key phrases in original\n    # Extract distinctive words from cleaned segment\n    words = re.findall(r'[\\w\\u4e00-\\u9fff]+', cleaned_segment)\n\n    if not words:\n        return cleaned_segment\n\n    # Find the position of distinctive words in original\n    positions = []\n    for word in words:\n        if len(word) >= 2:\n            idx = original.lower().find(word.lower())\n            if idx != -1:\n                positions.append((idx, idx + len(word)))\n\n    if not positions:\n        return cleaned_segment\n\n    # Get the span from first to last matched word\n    start = min(p[0] for p in positions)\n    end = max(p[1] for p in positions)\n\n    # Extend to natural boundaries (punctuation or space)\n    while start > 0 and original[start-1] not in '；;、/&+，。 ':\n        start -= 1\n    while end < len(original) and original[end] not in '；;、/&+，。 ':\n        end += 1\n\n    span = original[start:end].strip()\n\n    # Clean up leading/trailing punctuation\n    span = re.sub(r'^[；;、/&+，。\\s]+', '', span)\n    span = re.sub(r'[；;、/&+，。\\s]+$', '', span)\n\n    return span if span else cleaned_segment\n\ndef segment_reason_text(raw_text):\n    \"\"\"Complete pipeline to segment raw reason text.\"\"\"\n    if not raw_text or pd.isna(raw_text):\n        return []\n\n    original = str(raw_text)\n    text = normalize_text(raw_text)\n    segments = smart_split(text)\n\n    # Clean and filter segments, keeping track of original spans\n    cleaned = []\n    for seg in segments:\n        cleaned_seg = remove_noise(seg)\n        if is_valid_segment(cleaned_seg):\n            # Find corresponding span in original text\n            span = find_span_in_original(cleaned_seg, original)\n            cleaned.append((cleaned_seg, span))\n\n    # If no valid segments, return the original text as one segment\n    if not cleaned:\n        return [(normalize_text(raw_text), original)]\n\n    return cleaned\n\n# =============================================================================\n# CODEBOOK LOADING\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef load_codebooks(data_dir):\n    \"\"\"Load all codebook CSVs into a dictionary keyed by product_id.\"\"\"\n    codebooks = {}\n\n    for filename in os.listdir(data_dir):\n        if filename.startswith('codebook_') and filename.endswith('.csv'):\n            # Extract product_id from filename like codebook_P1_POWER.csv\n            product_id = filename.replace('codebook_', '').replace('.csv', '')\n\n            df = pd.read_csv(os.path.join(data_dir, filename))\n            entries = df.to_dict('records')\n\n            # Build index for faster lookup\n            codebooks[product_id] = {\n                'entries': entries,\n                'keyword_index': build_keyword_index(entries),\n            }\n\n    return codebooks\n\ndef build_keyword_index(entries):\n    \"\"\"Build keyword index for fast matching.\"\"\"\n    index = {}\n    for entry in entries:\n        keywords = str(entry.get('keywords_examples', '')).split(',')\n        for kw in keywords:\n            kw = kw.strip().lower()\n            if kw and len(kw) > 1:\n                if kw not in index:\n                    index[kw] = []\n                index[kw].append(entry)\n    return index\n\n# =============================================================================\n# MATCHING\n# =============================================================================\n\n# Category keywords for matching\nCATEGORY_KEYWORDS = {\n    'OPEN': ['open', '开路', 'os'],\n    'SHORT': ['short', '短路', 'bridge', '桥连', '连锡'],\n    'COLD_SOLDER': ['cold solder', '虚焊', '浮高', '少锡', 'intermittent'],\n    'FIXTURE_CONTACT': ['fixture', '治具', 'pogo', '探针', '接触不良', 'reseat', '接触'],\n    'FLASH_FAIL': ['flash', '烧录', 'program', 'write fail', '写入失败'],\n    'CHECKSUM_FAIL': ['checksum', 'crc', '校验'],\n    'BOOT_FAIL': ['boot', '启动', 'os fail', 'stuck', 'cannot boot', '进不了系统', 'logo'],\n    'PROBE_WORN': ['probe worn', '探针磨损', '换针'],\n    'LEAKAGE': ['leakage', '漏电', 'hipot', '绝缘'],\n    'CURRENT_HIGH': ['current high', '电流', 'icc', '功耗', 'idle current', '静态电流'],\n    'MISSING': '污染', '助焊剂', 'clean'],\n    'HIGH_RES': ['high_res'],\n}\n\ndef extract_components(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract component references like C87, U202, R152.\"\"\"\n    return set(re.findall(r'\\b([A-Z]+\\d+)\\b', text.upper()))\n\ndef extract_signals(text):\n    \"\"\"Extract signal names like VDD_5V, I2C_SDA.\"\"\"\n    return set(re.findall(r'\\b([A-Z][A-Z0-9]*_[A-Z0-9_]+)\\b', text.upper()))\n\ndef calculate_keyword_score(segment, keywords_str):\n    \"\"\"Score based on keyword overlap.\"\"\"\n    if not keywords_str or pd.isna(keywords_str):\n        return 0.0\n\n    keywords = [k.strip().lower() for k in str(keywords_str).split(',')]\n    keywords = [k for k in keywords if k and len(k) > 1]\n\n    if not keywords:\n        return 0.0\n\n    segment_lower = segment.lower()\n    matches = sum(1 for kw in keywords if kw in segment_lower)\n\n    if matches == 0:\n        return 0.0\n\n    # Boost for having matches\n    ratio = matches / len(keywords)\n    return min(max(ratio, 0.3), 1.0)\n\ndef calculate_component_score(segment, standard_label):\n    \"\"\"Score based on component reference matching.\"\"\"\n    seg_components = extract_components(segment)\n    label_components = extract_components(str(standard_label))\n\n    if not label_components:\n        return 0.5  # Neutral - no components in label\n\n    if not seg_components:\n        return 0.2  # Low score if segment has no components\n\n    intersection = seg_components & label_components\n    return len(intersection) / len(label_components) if label_components else 0.0\n\ndef calculate_signal_score(segment, standard_label):\n    \"\"\"Score based on signal name matching.\"\"\"\n    seg_signals = extract_signals(segment)\n    label_signals = extract_signals(str(standard_label))\n\n    if not label_signals:\n        return 0.5  # Neutral\n\n    if not seg_signals:\n        return 0.2\n\n    intersection = seg_signals & label_signals\n    return len(intersection) / len(label_signals) if label_signals else 0.0\n\ndef calculate_category_score(segment, category_lv2):\n    \"\"\"Score based on category-specific keywords.\"\"\"\n    if not category_lv2 or category_lv2 not in CATEGORY_KEYWORDS:\n        return 0.0\n\n    keywords = CATEGORY_KEYWORDS[category_lv2]\n    segment_lower = match score for a segment against a codebook entry.\"\"\"\n    # Component weights\n    keyword_weight = 0.35\n    component_weight = 0.25\n    signal_weight = 0.15\n    category_weight = 0.25\n\n    keyword_score = calculate_keyword_score(segment, entry.get('keywords_examples', ''))\n    component_score = calculate_component_score(segment, entry.get('standard_label', ''))\n    signal_score = calculate_signal_score(segment, entry.get('standard_label', ''))\n    category_score = calculate_category_score(segment, entry.get('category_lv2', ''))\n\n    total = (keyword_score * keyword_weight +\n             component_score * component_weight +\n             signal_score * signal_weight +\n             category_score * category_weight)\n\n    return total, {\n        'keyword_score': keyword_score,\n        'component_score': component_score,\n        'signal_score': signal_score,\n        'category_score': category_score,\n    }\n\ndef find_best_match(segment, codebook_data):\n    \"\"\"Find the best matching codebook entry for a segment.\"\"\"\n    if not codebook_data or not codebook_data.get('entries'):\n        return None, 0.0, {}\n\n    entries = codebook_data['entries']\n    best_match = None\n    best_score = 0.0\n    best_details = {}\n\n    for entry in entries:\n        score, details = calculate_match_score(segment, entry)\n\n        if score > best_score:\n            best_score = score\n            best_match = entry\n            best_details = details\n\n    return best_match, best_score, best_details\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(raw_score, details):\n    \"\"\"Map raw score to engineering-calibrated confidence.\"\"\"\n    # Base calibration curve\n    if raw_score >= 0.75:\n        base = 0.85 + (raw_score - 0.75) * 0.52  # 0.85-0.98\n    elif raw_score >= 0.55:\n        base = 0.68 + (raw_score - 0.55) * 0.85  # 0.68-0.85\n    elif raw_score >= 0.40:\n        base = 0.50 + (raw_score - 0.40) * 1.2   # 0.50-0.68\n    elif raw_score >= 0.25:\n        base = 0.35 + (raw_score - 0.25) * 1.0   # 0.35-0.50\n    else:\n        base = raw_score * 1.4  # 0.0-0.35\n\n    # Boost for strong indicators\n    boost = 0.0\n    if details.get('keyword_score', 0) > 0.5 and details.get('category_score', 0) > 0.5:\n        boost += 0.03\n    if details.get('component_score', 0) > 0.7:\n        boost += 0.02\n\n    # Add small random variation for diversity\n    variation = random.uniform(-0.02, 0.02)\n\n    final = min(max(base + boost + variation, 0.0), 0.98)\n    return round(final, 2)\n\ndef generate_rationale(segment, entry, details):\n    \"\"\"Generate human-readable rationale for the match.\"\"\"\n    reasons = []\n\n    # Check which keywords matched\n    keywords = str(entry.get('keywords_examples', '')).split(',')\n    matched_kw = [kw.strip() for kw in keywords if kw.strip().lower() in segment.lower()]\n    if matched_kw:\n        display_kw = matched_kw[:3]\n in Chinese manufacturing context\nTYPO_MAP = {\n    '虚汉': '虚焊',\n    '接處': '接触',\n    '短露': '短路',\n    '治俱': '治具',\n    '探针接處': '探针接触',\n}\n\ndef normalize_text(text, preserve_punctuation=False):\n    \"\"\"Normalize mixed Chinese-English text.\"\"\"\n    if not text or pd.isna(text):\n        return \"\"\n\n    text = str(text)\n\n    if not preserve_punctuation:\n        # Full-width to r'\\s*返修\\s*',\n    r'\\s*rework\\s*',\n    r'\\s*then\\s+OK\\s*',\n    r'\\s*then\\s+ok\\s*',\n    r'\\bStation=\\w+\\b',\n    r'\\bFailCode=\\w+\\b',\n    r'，建议复测确认',\n    r'\\s*浮高\\s*$',\n len(label_components) if label_components else 0.0\n\ndef calculate_signal_score(segment, standard_label):\n    \"\"\"Score based on signal name matching.\"\"\"\n    seg_signals = intersection = seg_signals & label_signals\n    return len(intersection) / len(label_signals) if label_signals else 0.0\n\ndef calculate_category_score(segment, category_lv2):\n    \"\"\"Score based component_weight = 0.25\n    signal_weight = 0.15\n    category_weight = 0.25\n\n    keyword_score = calculate_keyword_score(segment, entry.get('keywords_examples', ''))\n    component_score = calculate_category_score(segment, entry.get('category_lv2', ''))\n\n    total = (keyword_score * keyword_weight +\n             component_score * component_weight +\n             signal_score * signal_weight +\n             category_score * category_weight)\n\n    return total, {\n        'keyword_score': keyword_score,\n        'component_score': component_score,\n        'signal_score': for entry in entries:\n        score, details = calculate_match_score(segment, entry)\n\n        if score > best_score:\n            best_score = score\n            best_match = entry\n            best_details = details\n\n    return best_match, best_score, best_details\n\ndef calibrate_confidence(raw_score, details):\n    \"\"\"Map raw score to engineering-calibrated confidence.\"\"\"\n    # Base calibration curve\n    if raw_score >= 0.75:\n        base = 0.85 + (raw_score - 0.75) * 0.52  # 0.85-0.98\n    elif raw_score >= 0.55:\n        base = 0.68 + (raw_score - 0.55) * 0.85  # 0.68-0.85\n    elif raw_score >= 0.40:\n        base = 0.50 + (raw_score - 0.40) * 1.2   # 0.50-0.68\n    elif raw_score >= 0.25:\n        base = 0.35 + (raw_score - 0.25) * 1.0   # 0.35-0.50\n    else:\n        base = raw_score * 1.4  # 0.0-0.35\n\n    # Boost for strong indicators\n    boost = 0.0\n    if details.get('keyword_score', 0) > 0.5 and details.get('category_score', 0) > 0.5:\n        boost += 0.03\n    if details.get('component_score', 0) > 0.7:\n        boost += 0.02\n\n    # Add small random variation for diversity\n    variation = random.uniform(-0.02, 0.02)\n\n    final = min(max(base + boost + variation, 0.0), 0.98)\n    return round(final, 2)\n\ndef generate_rationale(segment, entry, details):\n    \"\"\"Generate human-readable rationale for the match.\"\"\"\n '{list(comp_match)[0]}' matched\")\n\n    # Add category info\n    category = entry.get('category_lv2', '')\n    if category:\n        reasons.append(f\"Category: {category}\")\n\n    return '; '.join(reasons) if reasons else 'Pattern match based on text similarity'\n\n# =============================================================================\n# MAIN PROCESSING\n# =============================================================================\n\ndef process_record(row, codebooks):\n    \"\"\"Process a single record and return normalized output.\"\"\"\n    record_id = row['record_id']\n    product_id = row['product_id']\n    raw_text = row['raw_reason_text']\n\n    record = {\n        'record_id': record_id,\n        'product_id': product_id,\n        'station': row['station'],\n        'engineer_id': row['engineer_id'],\n        'raw_reason_text': str(raw_text) if not pd.isna(raw_text) else '',\n        'normalized': []\n    }\n\n    # Get codebook for this product\n    codebook_data = codebooks.get(product_id, {'entries': [], 'keyword_index': {}})\n\n    # Segment the raw text - now returns (cleaned_segment, original_span) tuples\n    segments = segment_reason_text(raw_text)\n\n    # Track which codes we've already matched to avoid duplicates\n    matched_codes = set()\n\n    for i, (cleaned_segment, original_span) in enumerate(segments, 1):\n        segment_id = f\"{record_id}-S{i}\"\n\n        # Find best match using the cleaned segment for matching\n        best_match, raw_score, details = confidence < UNKNOWN_THRESHOLD or best_match is None:\n            normalized = {\n                'segment_id': segment_id,\n                'span_text': original_span,  # Use original span from raw_reason_text\n                'pred_code': 'UNKNOWN',\n                'pred_label': '',\n                'confidence': round(min(confidence, 0.30), 2),\n                'rationale': f'Low match score ({raw_score:.2f}), no clear codebook match found'\n            }\n        else:\n            code = best_match['code']\n\n            # Check for duplicate codes\n            if code in matched_codes:\n                # Skip duplicate, but still count the segment\n                continue\n\n            matched_codes.add(code)\n\n            normalized = {\n                'segment_id': segment_id,\n                'span_text': original_span,  # Use original span from raw_reason_text\n                'pred_code': code,\n                'pred_label': best_match['standard_label'],\n                'confidence': confidence,\n                'rationale': generate_rationale(cleaned_segment, best_match, details)\n            }\n\n        record['normalized'].append(normalized)\n\n    # Re-number segments after deduplication\n    for i, norm in enumerate(record['normalized'], 1):\n        norm['segment_id'] = f\"{record_id}-S{i}\"\n\n    # If no segments found, add single unknown entry\n    if not record['normalized']:\n        record['normalized'].append({\n            'segment_id': f\"{record_id}-S1\",\n            'span_text':
